15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L16",null,{"data":{"week":"2026-W45","date_range":"11-02","days":["2026-11-02"],"stats":{"total":91,"starred":3,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":3,"synthetic_data":3,"scaling":1,"architecture":7,"efficient":9,"eval":15,"multimodal":4,"multimodal_gen":7,"post_train":10,"mechanism":17,"agent":15}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","papers":[{"id":"2610.38312","title":"Robust LLM-Generated Text Detection through Matched Conditional-Response Probing","abstract":"Reliable detection of large language model (LLM)-generated text must remain effective under meaning-preserving text revisions, such as rewriting, polishing, and expansion. In practice, LLM outputs are often rewritten, polished, expanded, translated, or lightly edited before publication, weakening the lexical, syntactic, and local discourse cues used by many existing detectors. We introduce \\textbf{M}atched \\textbf{C}onditional-\\textbf{R}esponse \\textbf{P}robing (\\textbf{MCRP}), a frozen-LLM prob","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"glance","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-28T05:53:36Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","research_focus":{"version":1,"tracks":["pretraining_data"],"training_stages":["pretrain","midtrain"],"stage_basis":"text_hint","stage_evidence":[{"stage":"pretrain","field":"summary_en.experiments","quote":"goals for rewritten, polished, expanded, translated, and lightly edited text. There is no pretraining-from-scratch, continued-pretraining, or synthetic-token training experiment described."},{"stage":"midtrain","field":"summary.experiments","quote":"露具体数据集、probe LLM 名称、模型规模、baseline 列表或数值结果；只说明目标是对改写、润色、扩写、翻译和轻编辑场景保持有效。没有预训练 from-scratch、continued pretraining 或合成 token 进入训练的实验。"},{"stage":"pretrain","field":"summary.experiments","quote":"摘要未披露具体数据集、probe LLM 名称、模型规模、baseline 列表或数值结果；只说明目标是对改写、润色、扩写、翻译和轻编辑场景保持有效。没有预训练 from-scratch、continued pretraining 或合成 token 进入训练的实验。"}],"synthetic_methods":[]},"cover_url":"/covers/2610_38312.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"arxiv_url":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38312","source_url":null,"summary":{"core_problem":"检测经过改写、润色、扩写、翻译或轻编辑后的 LLM 生成文本，避免传统检测器依赖浅层词法和句法线索而失效。","importance":"贡献类型是 AI-generated text detection 方法。它不是预训练数据论文，但对预训练语料治理中的合成文本污染过滤有边际参考价值，尤其是检测器在 meaning-preserving revision 下的鲁棒性。","technical_approach":"提出 Matched Conditional-Response Probing。方法冻结一个 probe LLM，先为候选文本生成短语义条件，再比较 matched、null 和 control 条件下的 token-level loss 与 prompt-target attention；随后聚合双向条件对齐、反事实敏感性和内部 attention coupling，训练一个检测表示或分类器。核心思路是测量文本在语义条件变化下的响应，而不是只看表面风格。","experiments":"摘要未披露具体数据集、probe LLM 名称、模型规模、baseline 列表或数值结果；只说明目标是对改写、润色、扩写、翻译和轻编辑场景保持有效。没有预训练 from-scratch、continued pretraining 或合成 token 进入训练的实验。","authors_note":"作者来自 Sichuan University 的 School of Cyber Science and Engineering 和 Cyber Science Research Institute。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets detection of LLM-generated text after rewriting, polishing, expansion, translation, or light editing, where surface-level detectors often fail.","importance":"The contribution is a method for AI-generated text detection. It is not a pretraining-data paper in the strict sense, but it is marginally relevant to synthetic-text contamination filtering for pretraining corpora, especially under meaning-preserving revisions.","technical_approach":"The authors propose Matched Conditional-Response Probing. A frozen probe LLM generates a short semantic condition for each candidate and compares token-level losses and prompt-target attention under matched, null, and control conditions. It aggregates bidirectional conditioned alignment, counterfactual sensitivity, and internal attention coupling into a representation for detection.
